 407+ 6C44                      INCLUDE "src/kernel/p127_layout.asm"
# file opened: p127_layout.asm
   1++6C44              ; ============================================================================
   2++6C44              ; Project127 ATM Core — linked kernel image layout measurement
   3++6C44              ; Module version: 0.1.0
   4++6C44              ;
   5++6C44              ; Measures the actual assembled Stage2 kernel span [p127_entry,p127_end).
   6++6C44              ; It is not a fixed reservation: the linked end label determines the size.
   7++6C44              ; The current loader contract places the image at 6000h and bounds it by A000h.
   8++6C44              ; Both live host pages used by that CPU window (P5 and P2) must be RESERVED.
   9++6C44              ; This module validates placement only; it does not relocate or map pages.
  10++6C44              ; ============================================================================
  11++6C44              P127_LAYOUT_VERSION_MAJOR EQU 0
  12++6C44              P127_LAYOUT_VERSION_MINOR EQU 1
  13++6C44              P127_LAYOUT_VERSION_PATCH EQU 0
  14++6C44              P127_LAYOUT_START         EQU 06000h
  15++6C44              P127_LAYOUT_LIMIT         EQU 0A000h
  16++6C44              P127_LAYOUT_MAX_BYTES     EQU P127_LAYOUT_LIMIT-P127_LAYOUT_START
  17++6C44
  18++6C44              p127_layout_selftest:
  19++6C44                      ; The linked entry must match the BASIC loader's CODE address.
  20++6C44 21 00 60             ld      hl,p127_entry
  21++6C47 11 00 60             ld      de,P127_LAYOUT_START
  22++6C4A B7                   or      a
  23++6C4B ED 52                sbc     hl,de
  24++6C4D C2 8C 6C             jp      nz,p127_layout_reject

   1++6CE0              ; ============================================================================
   2++6CE0              ; Project127 — resident-window contract / software preflight
   3++6CE0              ; Module version: 0.4.0
   4++6CE0              ; Defines the only first-stage remap candidate without touching hardware.
   5++6CE0              ; W1/P5 owns resident execution+data; W2/P2 owns resident stack/control.
   6++6CE0              ; W3 is the only candidate target and may be used only after UI quiescence.
   7++6CE0              ; W0 is ROM. No OUT instruction is permitted here.
   8++6CE0              ; ============================================================================
   9++6CE0              P127_RESIDENT_VERSION_MAJOR EQU 0
  10++6CE0              P127_RESIDENT_VERSION_MINOR EQU 4
  11++6CE0              P127_RESIDENT_VERSION_PATCH EQU 0
  12++6CE0
  13++6CE0              ; The remap-critical resident module is deliberately placed first after the
  14++6CE0              ; Stage2 boot body. W1/P5 is the fixed execution/data window; W2/P2 is the
  15++6CE0              ; fixed stack window; W3 is the only target. This preflight checks the linked
  16++6CE0              ; addresses and current live stack at runtime. It performs no page OUT.
  17++6CE0              p127_resident_start:
  18++6CE0
  19++6CE0              P127_RESIDENT_CODE_WINDOW  EQU 1
  20++6CE0              P127_RESIDENT_CODE_PAGE    EQU 5
  21++6CE0              P127_RESIDENT_STACK_WINDOW EQU 2
  22++6CE0              P127_RESIDENT_STACK_PAGE   EQU 2
  23++6CE0              P127_RESIDENT_TARGET       EQU 3
  24++6CE0              P127_RESIDENT_UI_QUIESCED  EQU 1
  25++6CE0
  26++6CE0              ; IN: A=target window, B=resident code window, C=resident stack window,
  27++6CE0              ;     D=flags (bit 0 set means UI is quiesced).
  28++6CE0              ; OUT: carry=0 if the fixed resident contract is satisfied; carry=1 otherwise.
  29++6CE0              ; This is a software contract check, not a physical mapping operation.
  30++6CE0              p127_resident_validate:
  31++6CE0 32 6A 6D             ld      (p127_resident_arg_target),a
  32++6CE3 78                   ld      a,b
  33++6CE4 32 6B 6D             ld      (p127_resident_arg_code),a
  34++6CE7 79                   ld      a,c
  35++6CE8 32 6C 6D             ld      (p127_resident_arg_stack),a
  36++6CEB 7A                   ld      a,d
  37++6CEC 32 6D 6D             ld      (p127_resident_arg_flags),a
  38++6CEF
  39++6CEF 3A 6A 6D             ld      a,(p127_resident_arg_target)
  40++6CF2 FE 03                cp      P127_RESIDENT_TARGET
  41++6CF4 C2 1E 6D             jp      nz,p127_resident_reject

  54++6D14 CA 1E 6D             jp      z,p127_resident_reject

  60++6D1E              p127_resident_reject:
  61++6D1E 37                   scf
  62++6D1F C9                   ret

 348++6F11              ; ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
 349++6F11              ; Stage 2.10 — real-address resident trampoline preflight (software only).
 350++6F11              ; Requires DI on entry; DI is asserted again and interrupts remain disabled.
 351++6F11              ; Z80 does not provide a general instruction to read/restore prior IFF1, so
 352++6F11              ; this entry contract intentionally does not attempt an EI/IFF round trip.
 353++6F11              ; W1 is #4000-#7FFF; W2 is #8000-#BFFF. The linked resident module must fit
 354++6F11              ; wholly in W1, all resident state is inside that module, and the live stack
 355++6F11              ; must be in W2. No page register is written.
 356++6F11              p127_resident_trampoline_preflight:
 357++6F11 F3                   di
 358++6F12                      ; Linked code and data must remain wholly inside the fixed W1 window.
 359++6F12 21 E0 6C             ld      hl,p127_resident_start
 360++6F15 11 00 60             ld      de,06000h
 361++6F18 B7                   or      a
 362++6F19 ED 52                sbc     hl,de
 363++6F1B DA 5E 6F             jp      c,p127_resident_trampoline_fail
 364++6F1E
 365++6F1E 21 60 6F             ld      hl,p127_resident_end
 366++6F21 11 00 80             ld      de,08000h
 367++6F24 B7                   or      a
 368++6F25 ED 52                sbc     hl,de
 369++6F27 D2 5E 6F             jp      nc,p127_resident_trampoline_fail
 370++6F2A
 371++6F2A                      ; Representative mutable state must be within the resident module.
 372++6F2A 21 0B 6F             ld      hl,p127_w3_state
 373++6F2D 11 E0 6C             ld      de,p127_resident_start
 374++6F30 B7                   or      a
 375++6F31 ED 52                sbc     hl,de
 376++6F33 DA 5E 6F             jp      c,p127_resident_trampoline_fail
 377++6F36
 378++6F36 21 0B 6F             ld      hl,p127_w3_state
 379++6F39 11 60 6F             ld      de,p127_resident_end
 380++6F3C B7                   or      a
 381++6F3D ED 52                sbc     hl,de
 382++6F3F D2 5E 6F             jp      nc,p127_resident_trampoline_fail
 383++6F42
 384++6F42                      ; CALL has already pushed its return address. The live stack must
 385++6F42                      ; still be within W2, independent of W3's mapping.

 388++6F46 11 00 B0             ld      de,0B000h

   1++6F60              ; ============================================================================
   2++6F60              ; Project127 — interrupt safety contract / preflight
   3++6F60              ; Module version: 0.5.0
   4++6F60              ; This is deliberately a DI-only bootstrap contract, not an IRQ dispatcher.
   5++6F60              ; No EI, IM instruction, vector write, or hardware page OUT is permitted.
   6++6F60              ; ============================================================================
   7++6F60              P127_IRQ_VERSION_MAJOR EQU 0
   8++6F60              P127_IRQ_VERSION_MINOR EQU 5
   9++6F60              P127_IRQ_VERSION_PATCH EQU 0
  10++6F60              P127_IRQ_MASKED EQU 1
  11++6F60              P127_IRQ_VECTOR_NONE EQU 0
  12++6F60              P127_IRQ_MODE_UNSET EQU 0
  13++6F60
  14++6F60              ; Boot owns the CPU in a DI-protected phase. A stock Z80 cannot read IM0/1/2
  15++6F60              ; directly; LD A,I exposes IFF2 in P/V but is not a general IFF restore path.
  16++6F60              ; Therefore this module never guesses prior interrupt state and never EI/IM.
  17++6F60              ; Until a real vector table/handler is installed, vector ownership is NONE.
  18++6F60              ; Interrupts may not be enabled until all three prerequisites are explicitly
  19++6F60              ; established by later stages: writable/owned vector table, verified table
  20++6F60              ; mapping, and installed resident handler. No current boot code sets these.
  21++6F60              P127_IRQ_READY_VECTOR EQU 1
  22++6F60              P127_IRQ_READY_MAP EQU 2
  23++6F60              P127_IRQ_READY_HANDLER EQU 4
  24++6F60              P127_IRQ_READY_ALL EQU 7
  25++6F60
  26++6F60              p127_irq_init:
  27++6F60 F3                   di
  28++6F61 3E 01                ld      a,P127_IRQ_MASKED
  29++6F63 32 42 72             ld      (p127_irq_mask_state),a
  30++6F66 AF                   xor     a
  31++6F67 32 43 72             ld      (p127_irq_vector_owner),a
  32++6F6A 32 44 72             ld      (p127_irq_mode_policy),a
  33++6F6D 32 45 72             ld      (p127_irq_ready_flags),a
  34++6F70 B7                   or      a
  35++6F71 C9                   ret
  36++6F72
  37++6F72              ; OUT: carry clear only while the documented DI-only/no-vector contract holds.
  38++6F72              p127_irq_validate:
  39++6F72 3A 42 72             ld      a,(p127_irq_mask_state)
  40++6F75 FE 01                cp      P127_IRQ_MASKED
  41++6F77 C2 C9 70             jp      nz,p127_irq_reject
  42++6F7A 3A 43 72             ld      a,(p127_irq_vector_owner)
  43++6F7D FE 00                cp      P127_IRQ_VECTOR_NONE
  44++6F7F C2 C9 70             jp      nz,p127_irq_reject
  45++6F82 3A 44 72             ld      a,(p127_irq_mode_policy)
  46++6F85 FE 00                cp      P127_IRQ_MODE_UNSET
  47++6F87 C2 C9 70             jp      nz,p127_irq_reject
  48++6F8A B7                   or      a
  49++6F8B C9                   ret

  78++6FC5              ; and resident handler are all explicitly committed. This routine NEVER EI.

   1++7246              ; ============================================================================
   2++7246              ; Project127 ATM Core — physical page-state manager
   3++7246              ; Module version: 0.4.3
   4++7246              ; ABI: reserve A=first page, B=count; alloc -> A=page, C=exhausted;
   5++7246              ;      free A=page, C=invalid/not allocated.
   6++7246              ; Model: 64 physical 16 KiB page IDs = exactly 1 MiB.
   7++7246              ; ATM Turbo 2+ page IDs are physical 16 KiB RAM pages. Bootstrap reservations
   8++7246              ; reflect the active ZX-128 map: page 5 (window 1), page 2 (window 2), and
   9++7246              ; page 0 (window 3). Page 5 is already covered by the video-page union.
  10++7246              ; ============================================================================
  11++7246              P127_MEM_VERSION_MAJOR EQU 0
  12++7246              P127_MEM_VERSION_MINOR EQU 4
  13++7246              P127_MEM_VERSION_PATCH EQU 3
  14++7246              P127_MEM_PAGE_COUNT    EQU 64
  15++7246              P127_MEM_PAGE_SIZE     EQU 04000h
  16++7246              P127_MEM_FREE          EQU 0
  17++7246              P127_MEM_RESERVED      EQU 1
  18++7246              P127_MEM_ALLOCATED     EQU 2

   1++73C4              ; ============================================================================
   2++73C4              ; Project127 ATM Core — UINT32 physical range validator
   3++73C4              ; Module version: 0.1.2
   4++73C4              ; ABI:
   5++73C4              ;   p127_range_start  = 4-byte little-endian physical start address
   6++73C4              ;   p127_range_length = 4-byte little-endian byte length
   7++73C4              ;   call p127_range_validate; carry=1 if invalid, clear if valid; clobbers AF, B, DE, HL
   8++73C4              ; Semantics: half-open [start, start+length), bounded by 1 MiB.
   9++73C4              ; Zero-length ranges are valid when start <= 1 MiB.
  10++73C4              ; This is arithmetic/accounting only; it does not map or access RAM.
  11++73C4              ; ============================================================================
  12++73C4              P127_RANGE_VERSION_MAJOR EQU 0
  13++73C4              P127_RANGE_VERSION_MINOR EQU 1
  14++73C4              P127_RANGE_VERSION_PATCH EQU 2
  15++73C4              P127_RANGE_LIMIT         EQU 0100000h
  16++73C4
  17++73C4              p127_range_validate:
  18++73C4                      ; Add the unsigned 32-bit length to the unsigned start.
  19++73C4 21 D2 74             ld      hl,p127_range_start
  20++73C7 11 D6 74             ld      de,p127_range_length
  21++73CA 1A                   ld      a,(de)
  22++73CB 47                   ld      b,a
  23++73CC 7E                   ld      a,(hl)
  24++73CD 80                   add     a,b
  25++73CE 32 DA 74             ld      (p127_range_end),a
  26++73D1 23                   inc     hl
  27++73D2 13                   inc     de
  28++73D3 1A                   ld      a,(de)
  29++73D4 47                   ld      b,a
  30++73D5 7E                   ld      a,(hl)
  31++73D6 88                   adc     a,b
  32++73D7 32 DB 74             ld      (p127_range_end+1),a
  33++73DA 23                   inc     hl
  34++73DB 13                   inc     de
  35++73DC 1A                   ld      a,(de)
  36++73DD 47                   ld      b,a
  37++73DE 7E                   ld      a,(hl)
  38++73DF 88                   adc     a,b
  39++73E0 32 DC 74             ld      (p127_range_end+2),a
  40++73E3 23                   inc     hl
  41++73E4 13                   inc     de
  42++73E5 1A                   ld      a,(de)
  43++73E6 47                   ld      b,a
  44++73E7 7E                   ld      a,(hl)
  45++73E8 88                   adc     a,b
  46++73E9 32 DD 74             ld      (p127_range_end+3),a
  47++73EC D8                   ret     c                       ; unsigned 32-bit addition overflow
  48++73ED
  49++73ED                      ; End-exclusive address must be <= 0x00100000.
  50++73ED 3A DD 74             ld      a,(p127_range_end+3)
  51++73F0 B7                   or      a
  52++73F1 C2 0F 74             jp      nz,p127_range_invalid
  53++73F4 3A DC 74             ld      a,(p127_range_end+2)
  54++73F7 FE 10                cp      010h
  55++73F9 DA 0D 74             jp      c,p127_range_valid
  56++73FC C2 0F 74             jp      nz,p127_range_invalid
  57++73FF 3A DB 74             ld      a,(p127_range_end+1)
  58++7402 B7                   or      a
  59++7403 C2 0F 74             jp      nz,p127_range_invalid
  60++7406 3A DA 74             ld      a,(p127_range_end)
  61++7409 B7                   or      a
  62++740A C2 0F 74             jp      nz,p127_range_invalid
  63++740D              p127_range_valid:
  64++740D B7                   or      a
  65++740E C9                   ret
  66++740F              p127_range_invalid:
  67++740F 37                   scf
  68++7410 C9                   ret
  69++7411
  70++7411              ; Regression tests exercise the 1 MiB boundary, zero length and uint32 wrap.
  71++7411              ; OUT: carry=1 on any failed assertion.
  72++7411              p127_range_selftest:
  73++7411                      ; [0, 0) is valid.
  74++7411 AF                   xor     a

  77++7416 23                   inc     hl

   1++74DE              ; ============================================================================
   2++74DE              ; Project127 ATM Core — top-down physical program-page arena
   3++74DE              ; Module version: 0.1.2
   4++74DE              ;
   5++74DE              ; Arena discovers the highest contiguous FREE physical run from the page-state
   6++74DE              ; map. The current reservation map yields P8..P63; P6 remains isolated below
   7++74DE              ; reserved P7. Physical pages are 16 KiB and P63 ends at 0x100000.
   8++74DE              ;
   9++74DE              ; API:
  10++74DE              ;   p127_arena_init: discovers and validates the highest contiguous FREE run.
  11++74DE              ;   p127_arena_alloc: IN B=page count; OUT A=first page, carry=1 on failure.
  12++74DE              ;                    Allocates downward from the current high boundary.
  13++74DE              ;   p127_arena_free: IN A=first page, B=count; only most-recent block accepted.
  14++74DE              ;                   Frees it and contracts the high boundary upward.
  15++74DE              ;
  16++74DE              ; This is page-granular accounting only. It does not map pages, access RAM,
  17++74DE              ; provide byte-granular allocation, or promise a seamless legacy 16-bit ABI.
  18++74DE              ; Free is intentionally LIFO until per-process ownership/coalescing exists.
  19++74DE              ; ============================================================================
  20++74DE              P127_ARENA_VERSION_MAJOR EQU 0
  21++74DE              P127_ARENA_VERSION_MINOR EQU 1
  22++74DE              P127_ARENA_VERSION_PATCH EQU 2
  23++74DE              P127_ARENA_HIGH_PAGE      EQU 64
  24++74DE              P127_ARENA_PAGE_SIZE      EQU 04000h
  25++74DE
  26++74DE              ; Discover the highest contiguous FREE run from the actual page-state map.
  27++74DE              ; The first RESERVED/ALLOCATED page below that run is the lower guard.
  28++74DE              ; An all-FREE map is rejected: required system reservations are missing.
  29++74DE              p127_arena_init:
  30++74DE 21 C3 73             ld      hl,p127_mem_page_state+P127_ARENA_HIGH_PAGE-1
  31++74E1 0E 3F                ld      c,P127_ARENA_HIGH_PAGE-1
  32++74E3 06 40                ld      b,P127_ARENA_HIGH_PAGE

  36++74E7 C2 F1 74             jp      nz,.p127_arena_init_guard_found
  37++74EA 2B                   dec     hl
  38++74EB 0D                   dec     c
  39++74EC 10 F7                djnz    .p127_arena_init_scan
  40++74EE C3 A4 75             jp      p127_arena_reject
  41++74F1              .p127_arena_init_guard_found:
  42++74F1 0C                   inc     c
  43++74F2 79                   ld      a,c
  44++74F3 FE 40                cp      P127_ARENA_HIGH_PAGE
  45++74F5 D2 A4 75             jp      nc,p127_arena_reject
  46++74F8 32 35 76             ld      (p127_arena_low_page),a
  47++74FB 3E 40                ld      a,P127_ARENA_HIGH_PAGE
  48++74FD 91                   sub     c
  49++74FE CA A4 75             jp      z,p127_arena_reject
  50++7501 32 36 76             ld      (p127_arena_page_count),a
  51++7504 3E 40                ld      a,P127_ARENA_HIGH_PAGE
  52++7506 32 37 76             ld      (p127_arena_next_page),a
  53++7509 B7                   or      a
  54++750A C9                   ret
  55++750B

  57++750B              ; Validate the complete block before changing any page state.

  72++7524 48                   ld      c,b

   1++763C              ; ============================================================================
   2++763C              ; Project127 ATM Core — managed logical address-space page table
   3++763C              ; Module version: 0.1.0
   4++763C              ;
   5++763C              ; 4 MiB logical space: 256 logical pages x 16 KiB. A logical page maps to one
   6++763C              ; allocator-owned physical ATM page (0..63). Translation metadata only:
   7++763C              ; no CPU-window mapping, physical byte access, IN/OUT or DMA.
   8++763C              ;
   9++763C              ; API:
  10++763C              ;   p127_as_init: clear all slots to FFh (unmapped).
  11++763C              ;   p127_as_bind: A=logical page 0..255, B=allocated physical page 0..63.
  12++763C              ;   p127_as_unbind: A=logical page; removes metadata, does not free RAM.
  13++763C              ;   p127_as_translate: p127_as_address (uint32 LE) -> A=physical page,
  14++763C              ;                      HL=14-bit page offset; carry=1 if invalid/unmapped.
  15++763C              ; Caller must unbind before releasing a physical page.
  16++763C              ; ============================================================================
  17++763C              P127_AS_VERSION_MAJOR EQU 0
  18++763C              P127_AS_VERSION_MINOR EQU 1
  19++763C              P127_AS_VERSION_PATCH EQU 0
  20++763C              P127_AS_LOGICAL_PAGE_COUNT EQU 256
  21++763C              P127_AS_PAGE_SIZE EQU 04000h
  22++763C              P127_AS_UNMAPPED EQU 0FFh
  23++763C
  24++763C              p127_as_init:
  25++763C 21 DD 77             ld      hl,p127_as_page_table
  26++763F 36 FF                ld      (hl),P127_AS_UNMAPPED
  27++7641 11 DE 77             ld      de,p127_as_page_table+1
  28++7644 01 FF 00             ld      bc,P127_AS_LOGICAL_PAGE_COUNT-1
  29++7647 ED B0                ldir
  30++7649 B7                   or      a
  31++764A C9                   ret
  32++764B
  33++764B              ; Bind an allocator-owned physical page. Physical aliases are forbidden.
  34++764B              p127_as_bind:

  40++7657 5F                   ld      e,a
  41++7658 16 00                ld      d,0

  44++765E 7E                   ld      a,(hl)
  45++765F FE 02                cp      P127_MEM_ALLOCATED
  46++7661 C2 EF 76             jp      nz,p127_as_reject
  47++7664

  49++7667 5F                   ld      e,a
  50++7668 16 00                ld      d,0

  79++7698 C9                   ret

  95++76AA              ; Page index = address >> 14; offset = address & 3FFFh.
  96++76AA              p127_as_translate:

 124++76D3 21 DD 77             ld      hl,p127_as_page_table
 125++76D6 19                   add     hl,de

 127++76D8 FE FF                cp      P127_AS_UNMAPPED
 128++76DA CA EF 76             jp      z,p127_as_reject
 129++76DD 32 D7 77             ld      (p127_as_result_page),a
 130++76E0
 131++76E0                      ; Return the low 14 address bits as the in-page offset.
 132++76E0 3A D3 77             ld      a,(p127_as_address)
 133++76E3 6F                   ld      l,a
 134++76E4 3A D4 77             ld      a,(p127_as_address+1)

 136++76E9 67                   ld      h,a
 137++76EA 3A D7 77             ld      a,(p127_as_result_page)
 138++76ED B7                   or      a
 139++76EE C9                   ret
 140++76EF
 141++76EF              p127_as_reject:
 142++76EF 37                   scf
 143++76F0 C9                   ret
 144++76F1
 145++76F1              ; Startup regression: adjacent logical pages, exact boundary, unmapped slot,
 146++76F1              ; duplicate physical alias, 4 MiB limit, unbind and physical-page release.

   1++7ABD              ; ============================================================================
   2++7ABD              ; Project127 — managed logical-span copy
   3++7ABD              ; Module version: 0.1.0
   4++7ABD              ;
   5++7ABD              ; Copies between non-overlapping mapped logical spans using the guarded W3
   6++7ABD              ; lease and a resident 64-byte bounce buffer. W1/P5 holds this code/buffer;
   7++7ABD              ; W2/P2 holds the live stack. No UI call occurs while W3 is leased.
   8++7ABD              ;
   9++7ABD              ; API inputs:
  10++7ABD              ;   p127_as_copy_source      uint32 little-endian
  11++7ABD              ;   p127_as_copy_destination uint32 little-endian
  12++7ABD              ;   p127_as_copy_length      uint32 little-endian
  13++7ABD              ; Outputs:
  14++7ABD              ;   carry clear = success; carry set = failure
  15++7ABD              ;   p127_as_copy_completed   exact bytes written before return
  16++7ABD              ;   p127_as_copy_error       0 success, 1 span, 2 overlap, 3 lease,
  17++7ABD              ;                            4 source map, 5 destination map, 6 lease restore
  18++7ABD              ; Overlap is rejected; this is not memmove. Unexpected runtime failures after
  19++7ABD              ; writes begin may leave a destination prefix modified.
  20++7ABD              ; ============================================================================
  21++7ABD              P127_AS_COPY_VERSION_MAJOR EQU 0
  22++7ABD              P127_AS_COPY_VERSION_MINOR EQU 1
  23++7ABD              P127_AS_COPY_VERSION_PATCH EQU 3
  24++7ABD              P127_AS_COPY_BUFFER_SIZE EQU 64
  25++7ABD              P127_AS_COPY_WINDOW_BASE EQU 0C000h
  26++7ABD
  27++7ABD              p127_as_copy:
  28++7ABD AF                   xor     a
  29++7ABE 32 BC 7F             ld      (p127_as_copy_error),a
  30++7AC1 32 B8 7F             ld      (p127_as_copy_completed),a
  31++7AC4 32 B9 7F             ld      (p127_as_copy_completed+1),a
  32++7AC7 32 BA 7F             ld      (p127_as_copy_completed+2),a
  33++7ACA 32 BB 7F             ld      (p127_as_copy_completed+3),a
  34++7ACD
  35++7ACD                      ; Validate source and destination completely before taking W3.
  36++7ACD 21 AC 7F             ld      hl,p127_as_copy_source
  37++7AD0 11 A9 7A             ld      de,p127_as_span_start
  38++7AD3 01 04 00             ld      bc,4
  39++7AD6 ED B0                ldir
  40++7AD8 21 B4 7F             ld      hl,p127_as_copy_length
  41++7ADB 11 AD 7A             ld      de,p127_as_span_length
  42++7ADE 01 04 00             ld      bc,4
  43++7AE1 ED B0                ldir
  44++7AE3 CD DD 78             call    p127_as_span_check
  45++7AE6 DA CF 7C             jp      c,p127_as_copy_span_reject
  46++7AE9
  47++7AE9 21 B0 7F             ld      hl,p127_as_copy_destination
  48++7AEC 11 A9 7A             ld      de,p127_as_span_start
  49++7AEF 01 04 00             ld      bc,4
  50++7AF2 ED B0                ldir
  51++7AF4 CD DD 78             call    p127_as_span_check
  52++7AF7 DA CF 7C             jp      c,p127_as_copy_span_reject
  53++7AFA
  54++7AFA                      ; Empty spans are valid no-ops, including an address exactly 4 MiB.
  55++7AFA 3A B4 7F             ld      a,(p127_as_copy_length)
  56++7AFD 47                   ld      b,a
  57++7AFE 3A B5 7F             ld      a,(p127_as_copy_length+1)
  58++7B01 B0                   or      b
  59++7B02 47                   ld      b,a
  60++7B03 3A B6 7F             ld      a,(p127_as_copy_length+2)
  61++7B06 B0                   or      b
  62++7B07 47                   ld      b,a
  63++7B08 3A B7 7F             ld      a,(p127_as_copy_length+3)
  64++7B0B B0                   or      b
  65++7B0C CA DD 7C             jp      z,p127_as_copy_success
  66++7B0F
  67++7B0F                      ; Calculate both end-exclusive addresses (preflight proved no overflow).
  68++7B0F 2A AC 7F             ld      hl,(p127_as_copy_source)
  69++7B12 3A B4 7F             ld      a,(p127_as_copy_length)
  70++7B15 4F                   ld      c,a
  71++7B16 3A B5 7F             ld      a,(p127_as_copy_length+1)
  72++7B19 47                   ld      b,a
  73++7B1A 09                   add     hl,bc
  74++7B1B 22 BD 7F             ld      (p127_as_copy_source_end),hl
  75++7B1E 2A AE 7F             ld      hl,(p127_as_copy_source+2)
  76++7B21 3A B6 7F             ld      a,(p127_as_copy_length+2)
  77++7B24 4F                   ld      c,a
  78++7B25 3A B7 7F             ld      a,(p127_as_copy_length+3)
  79++7B28 47                   ld      b,a
  80++7B29 ED 4A                adc     hl,bc
  81++7B2B 22 BF 7F             ld      (p127_as_copy_source_end+2),hl
  82++7B2E
  83++7B2E 2A B0 7F             ld      hl,(p127_as_copy_destination)
  84++7B31 3A B4 7F             ld      a,(p127_as_copy_length)
  85++7B34 4F                   ld      c,a
  86++7B35 3A B5 7F             ld      a,(p127_as_copy_length+1)
  87++7B38 47                   ld      b,a
  88++7B39 09                   add     hl,bc
  89++7B3A 22 C1 7F             ld      (p127_as_copy_destination_end),hl
  90++7B3D 2A B2 7F             ld      hl,(p127_as_copy_destination+2)
  91++7B40 3A B6 7F             ld      a,(p127_as_copy_length+2)
  92++7B43 4F                   ld      c,a
  93++7B44 3A B7 7F             ld      a,(p127_as_copy_length+3)
  94++7B47 47                   ld      b,a
  95++7B48 ED 4A                adc     hl,bc
  96++7B4A 22 C3 7F             ld      (p127_as_copy_destination_end+2),hl
  97++7B4D
  98++7B4D                      ; Disjoint iff source_end <= destination OR destination_end <= source.
  99++7B4D 21 BD 7F             ld      hl,p127_as_copy_source_end
 100++7B50 11 B0 7F             ld      de,p127_as_copy_destination
 101++7B53 CD E3 7C             call    p127_as_copy_compare_u32
 102++7B56 38 16                jr      c,p127_as_copy_ranges_disjoint
 103++7B58 28 14                jr      z,p127_as_copy_ranges_disjoint
 104++7B5A 21 C1 7F             ld      hl,p127_as_copy_destination_end
 105++7B5D 11 AC 7F             ld      de,p127_as_copy_source
 106++7B60 CD E3 7C             call    p127_as_copy_compare_u32
 107++7B63 38 09                jr      c,p127_as_copy_ranges_disjoint
 108++7B65 28 07                jr      z,p127_as_copy_ranges_disjoint
 109++7B67 3E 02                ld      a,2
 110++7B69 32 BC 7F             ld      (p127_as_copy_error),a
 111++7B6C 37                   scf
 112++7B6D C9                   ret
 113++7B6E
 114++7B6E              p127_as_copy_ranges_disjoint:
 115++7B6E                      ; W3 begin is the only transition into physical access.
 116++7B6E CD 75 A9             call    p127_physmem_w3_begin
 117++7B71 DA D6 7C             jp      c,p127_as_copy_lease_reject
 118++7B74
 119++7B74                      ; Remaining is the requested byte count, not the source address.
 120++7B74 21 B4 7F             ld      hl,p127_as_copy_length
 121++7B77 11 C5 7F             ld      de,p127_as_copy_remaining
 122++7B7A 01 04 00             ld      bc,4
 123++7B7D ED B0                ldir
 124++7B7F 21 AC 7F             ld      hl,p127_as_copy_source
 125++7B82 11 C9 7F             ld      de,p127_as_copy_work_source
 126++7B85 01 04 00             ld      bc,4
 127++7B88 ED B0                ldir
 128++7B8A 21 B0 7F             ld      hl,p127_as_copy_destination
 129++7B8D 11 CD 7F             ld      de,p127_as_copy_work_destination
 130++7B90 01 04 00             ld      bc,4
 131++7B93 ED B0                ldir
 132++7B95
 133++7B95              p127_as_copy_loop:
 134++7B95                      ; Remaining==0 completes the transfer.
 135++7B95 3A C5 7F             ld      a,(p127_as_copy_remaining)
 136++7B98 47                   ld      b,a
 137++7B99 3A C6 7F             ld      a,(p127_as_copy_remaining+1)
 138++7B9C B0                   or      b
 139++7B9D 47                   ld      b,a
 140++7B9E 3A C7 7F             ld      a,(p127_as_copy_remaining+2)
 141++7BA1 B0                   or      b
 142++7BA2 47                   ld      b,a
 143++7BA3 3A C8 7F             ld      a,(p127_as_copy_remaining+3)
 144++7BA6 B0                   or      b
 145++7BA7 CA A4 7C             jp      z,p127_as_copy_finish
 146++7BAA
 147++7BAA                      ; Translate the current source byte address.
 148++7BAA 21 C9 7F             ld      hl,p127_as_copy_work_source
 149++7BAD 11 D3 77             ld      de,p127_as_address
 150++7BB0 01 04 00             ld      bc,4
 151++7BB3 ED B0                ldir
 152++7BB5 CD AA 76             call    p127_as_translate
 153++7BB8 DA B0 7C             jp      c,p127_as_copy_source_map_fail
 154++7BBB 32 D1 7F             ld      (p127_as_copy_source_page),a
 155++7BBE 22 D3 7F             ld      (p127_as_copy_source_offset),hl
 156++7BC1
 157++7BC1                      ; Translate the current destination byte address.
 158++7BC1 21 CD 7F             ld      hl,p127_as_copy_work_destination
 159++7BC4 11 D3 77             ld      de,p127_as_address
 160++7BC7 01 04 00             ld      bc,4
 161++7BCA ED B0                ldir
 162++7BCC CD AA 76             call    p127_as_translate
 163++7BCF DA B8 7C             jp      c,p127_as_copy_destination_map_fail
 164++7BD2 32 D2 7F             ld      (p127_as_copy_destination_page),a
 165++7BD5 22 D5 7F             ld      (p127_as_copy_destination_offset),hl
 166++7BD8
 167++7BD8                      ; Chunk <= 64, remaining length, source page tail and destination tail.
 168++7BD8 3E 40                ld      a,P127_AS_COPY_BUFFER_SIZE
 169++7BDA 32 D7 7F             ld      (p127_as_copy_chunk),a
 170++7BDD 2A D3 7F             ld      hl,(p127_as_copy_source_offset)
 171++7BE0 CD 00 7D             call    p127_as_copy_limit_page_tail
 172++7BE3 2A D5 7F             ld      hl,(p127_as_copy_destination_offset)
 173++7BE6 CD 00 7D             call    p127_as_copy_limit_page_tail
 174++7BE9 3A C6 7F             ld      a,(p127_as_copy_remaining+1)
 175++7BEC 47                   ld      b,a
 176++7BED 3A C7 7F             ld      a,(p127_as_copy_remaining+2)
 177++7BF0 B0                   or      b
 178++7BF1 47                   ld      b,a
 179++7BF2 3A C8 7F             ld      a,(p127_as_copy_remaining+3)
 180++7BF5 B0                   or      b
 181++7BF6 20 10                jr      nz,p127_as_copy_chunk_ready
 182++7BF8                      ; Apply remaining as another MIN constraint; never enlarge a chunk
 183++7BF8                      ; already shortened by either logical-page boundary.
 184++7BF8 3A C5 7F             ld      a,(p127_as_copy_remaining)
 185++7BFB 47                   ld      b,a
 186++7BFC 3A D7 7F             ld      a,(p127_as_copy_chunk)
 187++7BFF B8                   cp      b
 188++7C00 38 06                jr      c,p127_as_copy_chunk_ready
 189++7C02 28 04                jr      z,p127_as_copy_chunk_ready
 190++7C04 78                   ld      a,b
 191++7C05 32 D7 7F             ld      (p127_as_copy_chunk),a
 192++7C08              p127_as_copy_chunk_ready:
 193++7C08 3A D1 7F             ld      a,(p127_as_copy_source_page)
 194++7C0B CD B0 A9             call    p127_physmem_w3_map
 195++7C0E DA B0 7C             jp      c,p127_as_copy_source_map_fail
 196++7C11
 197++7C11                      ; Read source bytes into the resident bounce buffer.
 198++7C11 2A D3 7F             ld      hl,(p127_as_copy_source_offset)
 199++7C14 11 00 C0             ld      de,P127_AS_COPY_WINDOW_BASE
 200++7C17 19                   add     hl,de
 201++7C18 11 D8 7F             ld      de,p127_as_copy_buffer
 202++7C1B 3A D7 7F             ld      a,(p127_as_copy_chunk)
 203++7C1E 4F                   ld      c,a
 204++7C1F 06 00                ld      b,0
 205++7C21 ED B0                ldir
 206++7C23
 207++7C23                      ; Map destination only after the source chunk is safely resident.
 208++7C23 3A D2 7F             ld      a,(p127_as_copy_destination_page)
 209++7C26 CD B0 A9             call    p127_physmem_w3_map
 210++7C29 DA B8 7C             jp      c,p127_as_copy_destination_map_fail
 211++7C2C 2A D5 7F             ld      hl,(p127_as_copy_destination_offset)
 212++7C2F 11 00 C0             ld      de,P127_AS_COPY_WINDOW_BASE
 213++7C32 19                   add     hl,de
 214++7C33 EB                   ex      de,hl
 215++7C34 21 D8 7F             ld      hl,p127_as_copy_buffer
 216++7C37 3A D7 7F             ld      a,(p127_as_copy_chunk)
 217++7C3A 4F                   ld      c,a
 218++7C3B 06 00                ld      b,0
 219++7C3D ED B0                ldir
 220++7C3F
 221++7C3F                      ; Advance both 32-bit cursors by this chunk.
 222++7C3F 3A D7 7F             ld      a,(p127_as_copy_chunk)
 223++7C42 4F                   ld      c,a
 224++7C43 06 00                ld      b,0
 225++7C45 2A C9 7F             ld      hl,(p127_as_copy_work_source)
 226++7C48 09                   add     hl,bc
 227++7C49 22 C9 7F             ld      (p127_as_copy_work_source),hl
 228++7C4C 2A CB 7F             ld      hl,(p127_as_copy_work_source+2)
 229++7C4F 11 00 00             ld      de,0
 230++7C52 ED 5A                adc     hl,de
 231++7C54 22 CB 7F             ld      (p127_as_copy_work_source+2),hl
 232++7C57
 233++7C57 3A D7 7F             ld      a,(p127_as_copy_chunk)
 234++7C5A 4F                   ld      c,a
 235++7C5B 06 00                ld      b,0
 236++7C5D 2A CD 7F             ld      hl,(p127_as_copy_work_destination)
 237++7C60 09                   add     hl,bc
 238++7C61 22 CD 7F             ld      (p127_as_copy_work_destination),hl
 239++7C64 2A CF 7F             ld      hl,(p127_as_copy_work_destination+2)
 240++7C67 11 00 00             ld      de,0
 241++7C6A ED 5A                adc     hl,de
 242++7C6C 22 CF 7F             ld      (p127_as_copy_work_destination+2),hl
 243++7C6F
 244++7C6F                      ; remaining -= chunk; completed += chunk.
 245++7C6F 3A D7 7F             ld      a,(p127_as_copy_chunk)
 246++7C72 4F                   ld      c,a
 247++7C73 06 00                ld      b,0
 248++7C75 B7                   or      a
 249++7C76 2A C5 7F             ld      hl,(p127_as_copy_remaining)
 250++7C79 ED 42                sbc     hl,bc
 251++7C7B 22 C5 7F             ld      (p127_as_copy_remaining),hl
 252++7C7E 2A C7 7F             ld      hl,(p127_as_copy_remaining+2)
 253++7C81 01 00 00             ld      bc,0
 254++7C84 ED 42                sbc     hl,bc
 255++7C86 22 C7 7F             ld      (p127_as_copy_remaining+2),hl
 256++7C89
 257++7C89 3A D7 7F             ld      a,(p127_as_copy_chunk)
 258++7C8C 4F                   ld      c,a
 259++7C8D 06 00                ld      b,0
 260++7C8F 2A B8 7F             ld      hl,(p127_as_copy_completed)
 261++7C92 09                   add     hl,bc
 262++7C93 22 B8 7F             ld      (p127_as_copy_completed),hl
 263++7C96 2A BA 7F             ld      hl,(p127_as_copy_completed+2)
 264++7C99 01 00 00             ld      bc,0
 265++7C9C ED 4A                adc     hl,bc
 266++7C9E 22 BA 7F             ld      (p127_as_copy_completed+2),hl
 267++7CA1 C3 95 7B             jp      p127_as_copy_loop
 268++7CA4
 269++7CA4              p127_as_copy_finish:
 270++7CA4 CD D6 A9             call    p127_physmem_w3_end
 271++7CA7 DA C8 7C             jp      c,p127_as_copy_restore_fail
 272++7CAA AF                   xor     a
 273++7CAB 32 BC 7F             ld      (p127_as_copy_error),a
 274++7CAE B7                   or      a
 275++7CAF C9                   ret
 276++7CB0
 277++7CB0              p127_as_copy_source_map_fail:
 278++7CB0 3E 04                ld      a,4
 279++7CB2 32 BC 7F             ld      (p127_as_copy_error),a
 280++7CB5 C3 C0 7C             jp      p127_as_copy_abort
 281++7CB8              p127_as_copy_destination_map_fail:
 282++7CB8 3E 05                ld      a,5
 283++7CBA 32 BC 7F             ld      (p127_as_copy_error),a
 284++7CBD C3 C0 7C             jp      p127_as_copy_abort
 285++7CC0              p127_as_copy_abort:
 286++7CC0 CD D6 A9             call    p127_physmem_w3_end
 287++7CC3 DA C8 7C             jp      c,p127_as_copy_restore_fail
 288++7CC6 37                   scf
 289++7CC7 C9                   ret
 290++7CC8              p127_as_copy_restore_fail:
 291++7CC8 3E 06                ld      a,6
 292++7CCA 32 BC 7F             ld      (p127_as_copy_error),a
 293++7CCD 37                   scf
 294++7CCE C9                   ret
 295++7CCF              p127_as_copy_span_reject:
 296++7CCF 3E 01                ld      a,1
 297++7CD1 32 BC 7F             ld      (p127_as_copy_error),a
 298++7CD4 37                   scf
 299++7CD5 C9                   ret
 300++7CD6              p127_as_copy_lease_reject:
 301++7CD6 3E 03                ld      a,3
 302++7CD8 32 BC 7F             ld      (p127_as_copy_error),a
 303++7CDB 37                   scf
 304++7CDC C9                   ret
 305++7CDD              p127_as_copy_success:
 306++7CDD AF                   xor     a
 307++7CDE 32 BC 7F             ld      (p127_as_copy_error),a
 308++7CE1 B7                   or      a
 309++7CE2 C9                   ret
 310++7CE3
 311++7CE3              ; IN HL=left uint32 pointer, DE=right uint32 pointer.
 312++7CE3              ; OUT carry if left<right; Z if equal. Preserves BC.
 313++7CE3              p127_as_copy_compare_u32:
 314++7CE3 C5                   push    bc
 315++7CE4 01 03 00             ld      bc,3
 316++7CE7 09                   add     hl,bc
 317++7CE8 EB                   ex      de,hl
 318++7CE9 09                   add     hl,bc
 319++7CEA 1A                   ld      a,(de)
 320++7CEB BE                   cp      (hl)
 321++7CEC 20 10                jr      nz,p127_as_copy_compare_done
 322++7CEE 1B                   dec     de
 323++7CEF 2B                   dec     hl
 324++7CF0 1A                   ld      a,(de)
 325++7CF1 BE                   cp      (hl)
 326++7CF2 20 0A                jr      nz,p127_as_copy_compare_done
 327++7CF4 1B                   dec     de
 328++7CF5 2B                   dec     hl
 329++7CF6 1A                   ld      a,(de)
 330++7CF7 BE                   cp      (hl)
 331++7CF8 20 04                jr      nz,p127_as_copy_compare_done
 332++7CFA 1B                   dec     de
 333++7CFB 2B                   dec     hl
 334++7CFC 1A                   ld      a,(de)
 335++7CFD BE                   cp      (hl)
 336++7CFE              p127_as_copy_compare_done:
 337++7CFE C1                   pop     bc
 338++7CFF C9                   ret
 339++7D00
 340++7D00              ; IN HL=14-bit page offset. Reduce current chunk if fewer than 64 bytes
 341++7D00              ; remain before the end of this 16 KiB logical page.
 342++7D00              p127_as_copy_limit_page_tail:
 343++7D00 7C                   ld      a,h
 344++7D01 FE 3F                cp      03Fh
 345++7D03 C0                   ret     nz
 346++7D04 7D                   ld      a,l
 347++7D05 FE C1                cp      0C1h
 348++7D07 D8                   ret     c
 349++7D08 AF                   xor     a
 350++7D09 95                   sub     l
 351++7D0A 47                   ld      b,a
 352++7D0B 3A D7 7F             ld      a,(p127_as_copy_chunk)
 353++7D0E B8                   cp      b
 354++7D0F D8                   ret     c
 355++7D10 78                   ld      a,b
 356++7D11 32 D7 7F             ld      (p127_as_copy_chunk),a
 357++7D14 C9                   ret
 358++7D15
 359++7D15              ; Runtime regression: seed two source pages, copy 32 bytes across both source
 360++7D15              ; and destination page boundaries, then verify destination byte-for-byte.
 361++7D15              p127_as_copy_selftest:
 362++7D15 3E 01                ld      a,1
 363++7D17 32 18 80             ld      (p127_as_copy_test_phase),a
 364++7D1A AF                   xor     a
 365++7D1B 32 1C 80             ld      (p127_as_copy_test_alloc_count),a
 366++7D1E 32 1D 80             ld      (p127_as_copy_test_bind_count),a
 367++7D21 32 1E 80             ld      (p127_as_copy_test_lease),a
 368++7D24 CD 3C 76             call    p127_as_init
 369++7D27
 370++7D27 CD 57 A9             call    p127_physmem_alloc_page
 371++7D2A DA 72 7F             jp      c,p127_as_copy_test_fail
 372++7D2D 32 19 80             ld      (p127_as_copy_test_page_a),a
 373++7D30 3E 01                ld      a,1
 374++7D32 32 1C 80             ld      (p127_as_copy_test_alloc_count),a
 375++7D35 CD 57 A9             call    p127_physmem_alloc_page
 376++7D38 DA 1D 7F             jp      c,p127_as_copy_test_cleanup
 377++7D3B 32 1A 80             ld      (p127_as_copy_test_page_b),a
 378++7D3E 3E 02                ld      a,2
 379++7D40 32 1C 80             ld      (p127_as_copy_test_alloc_count),a
 380++7D43 CD 57 A9             call    p127_physmem_alloc_page
 381++7D46 DA 1D 7F             jp      c,p127_as_copy_test_cleanup
 382++7D49 32 1B 80             ld      (p127_as_copy_test_page_c),a
 383++7D4C 3E 03                ld      a,3
 384++7D4E 32 1C 80             ld      (p127_as_copy_test_alloc_count),a
 385++7D51
 386++7D51 3A 19 80             ld      a,(p127_as_copy_test_page_a)
 387++7D54 47                   ld      b,a
 388++7D55 AF                   xor     a
 389++7D56 CD 4B 76             call    p127_as_bind
 390++7D59 DA 1D 7F             jp      c,p127_as_copy_test_cleanup
 391++7D5C 3E 01                ld      a,1
 392++7D5E 32 1D 80             ld      (p127_as_copy_test_bind_count),a
 393++7D61 3A 1A 80             ld      a,(p127_as_copy_test_page_b)
 394++7D64 47                   ld      b,a
 395++7D65 3E 01                ld      a,1
 396++7D67 CD 4B 76             call    p127_as_bind
 397++7D6A DA 1D 7F             jp      c,p127_as_copy_test_cleanup
 398++7D6D 3E 02                ld      a,2
 399++7D6F 32 1D 80             ld      (p127_as_copy_test_bind_count),a
 400++7D72 3A 1B 80             ld      a,(p127_as_copy_test_page_c)
 401++7D75 47                   ld      b,a
 402++7D76 3E 02                ld      a,2
 403++7D78 CD 4B 76             call    p127_as_bind
 404++7D7B DA 1D 7F             jp      c,p127_as_copy_test_cleanup
 405++7D7E 3E 03                ld      a,3
 406++7D80 32 1D 80             ld      (p127_as_copy_test_bind_count),a

 178++816B              ; Safe-remap preparation gate (software-only).
 179++816B              ; IN: A=target window 0..3, B=new physical page 0..63,
 180++816B              ;     C=bitmask of protected windows (bit 0=W0 ... bit 3=W3).
 181++816B              ; OUT: carry=1 if invalid/protected; carry=0 if a pending request is staged.
 182++816B              ; This routine NEVER writes a page port and NEVER changes the active map.
 183++816B              ; Caller must include execution, stack, UI, interrupt and host/staging windows
 184++816B              ; in C. The bootstrap currently protects all four windows (#0F).

   1++8361              ; ============================================================================
   2++8361              ; Project127 — resident stack handoff probe
   3++8361              ; Module version: 0.1.0
   4++8361              ; Runs from the resident Stage2 image in W1/P5. Scratch stack is a bounded
   5++8361              ; test area in the still-mapped W2/P2. No page port is written.
   6++8361              ; ============================================================================
   7++8361              P127_HANDOFF_VERSION_MAJOR EQU 0
   8++8361              P127_HANDOFF_VERSION_MINOR EQU 1
   9++8361              P127_HANDOFF_VERSION_PATCH EQU 0
  10++8361
  11++8361              ; Current bootstrap stack is at #BFFF in W2/P2. Keep at least 128 bytes
  12++8361              ; between its low-water boundary and the scratch stack.
  13++8361              P127_HANDOFF_MIN_OLD_SP    EQU 0B900h
  14++8361              P127_HANDOFF_STACK_BOTTOM  EQU 0B800h
  15++8361              P127_HANDOFF_STACK_TOP     EQU 0B880h
  16++8361              P127_HANDOFF_GUARD_LO      EQU 0B7FEh
  17++8361              P127_HANDOFF_GUARD_HI      EQU 0B7FFh
  18++8361
  19++8361              ; IN: none.
  20++8361              ; OUT: carry=1 on failure; carry=0 on success.
  21++8361              ; Preserves BC, DE, HL, IX and IY. AF/flags are return status.
  22++8361              ; Requires interrupts disabled. DI is asserted defensively and this routine
  23++8361              ; returns with interrupts disabled. IM and interrupt vectors are untouched.
  24++8361              ; The active stack must be at/above #B900 after this routine's save frame.
  25++8361              ; This is a stack-integrity probe only: W2 remains mapped throughout.
  26++8361              p127_handoff_stack_probe:

  28++8362 F5                   push    af

   1++83FC              ; ============================================================================
   2++83FC              ; Project127 TR-DOS directory reader
   3++83FC              ; Module version: 0.1.2
   4++83FC              ; Reads track 0 sectors 1..8 via the documented TR-DOS ROM entry #3D13.
   5++83FC              ; Directory snapshot: 128 * 16 bytes at #C800 (reserved host/staging page P0).
   6++83FC              ; Read-only: no file-data reads, writes, DMA, IM/EI or raw paging OUT.
   7++83FC              ; ============================================================================
   8++83FC              P127_TRDOS_VERSION_MAJOR EQU 0
   9++83FC              P127_TRDOS_VERSION_MINOR EQU 1
  10++83FC              P127_TRDOS_VERSION_PATCH EQU 2
  11++83FC              P127_TRDOS_DIRECTORY_BUFFER EQU 0C800h
  12++83FC              ; Separate from the live caller stack at #BFFF and handoff probe stack #B800.
  13++83FC              ; The 240-byte usable interval #B700..#B7EF is below the handoff canary.
  14++83FC              P127_TRDOS_SCRATCH_STACK_TOP EQU 0B7F0h
  15++83FC              P127_TRDOS_DIRECTORY_BYTES EQU 0800h
  16++83FC              P127_TRDOS_ENTRY_COUNT EQU 128
  17++83FC              P127_TRDOS_ENTRY_SIZE EQU 16
  18++83FC
  19++83FC              ; Public entry. Returns carry set on sector-read error.
  20++83FC              ; Output is mirrored by p127_puts/p127_put_char to the visible console and #EF.
  21++83FC              ; Called immediately after the Project126-compatible legacy mapping
  22++83FC              ; setup, before p127_ui_init changes the ROM/video/shadow-port state.

  44+ A015                      ; Reset the ATM paging/control latch BEFORE enabling permanent
  45+ A015                      ; DOSEN. On this board #EFF7 is part of the ATM paging control path;
  46+ A015                      ; resetting it after the #0177 shadow-port setup can undo the state
  47+ A015                      ; needed by the TR-DOS overlay.
  48+ A015 AF                   xor     a
  49+ A016 01 F7 EF             ld      bc, #EFF7
  50+ A019 ED 79                out     (c), a
  51+ A01B
  52+ A01B                      ; ROM2=1 (D4) is required for TR-DOS overlay; D3 selects alternate
  53+ A01B                      ; video pages 7/3. This is #18, matching the host display setup.
  54+ A01B 3E 18                ld      a, #18
  55+ A01D 01 FD 7F             ld      bc, #7FFD
  56+ A020 ED 79                out     (c), a
  57+ A022 32 3B 5C             ld      (#5C3B), a
  58+ A025
  59+ A025                      ; Open shadow ports and hold DOSEN permanently (A9=0) using the
  60+ A025                      ; documented TR-DOS ROM trampoline. Do this AFTER the paging reset.
  61+ A025 01 35 A0             ld      bc, p126_atm_ports_open_done
  62+ A028 C5                   push    bc
  63+ A029 01 53 2A             ld      bc, #2A53
  64+ A02C C5                   push    bc
  65+ A02D 01 77 01             ld      bc, #0177
  66+ A030 3E C3                ld      a, #C3
  67+ A032 C3 2F 3D             jp      #3D2F
  68+ A035
  69+ A035              p126_atm_ports_open_done:
  70+ A035                      ; Window 0 must use ROM with D7=1 so its ROM page selection is
  71+ A035                      ; coupled to ROM2/DOSEN. #BF selects the low ROM page with the
  72+ A035                      ; DOSEN-controlled low ROM select: BASIC-48 when DOSEN=0, TR-DOS
  73+ A035                      ; when DOSEN=1. This is the ATM2+ documented auto-TR-DOS mapping.
  74+ A035 3E BF                ld      a, #BF
  75+ A037 01 F7 3F             ld      bc, #3FF7
  76+ A03A ED 79                out     (c), a
  77+ A03C
  78+ A03C
  79+ A03C                      ; Map the alternate screen pages while the 32-byte mode trampoline
  80+ A03C                      ; is installed at #FFEC. Window 3 uses a fixed RAM page here; the
  81+ A03C                      ; TR-DOS auto-select mux is configured separately in window 0.
  82+ A03C 3E 78                ld      a, P126_VIDEO_PAGE7
  83+ A03E 01 F7 7F             ld      bc, #7FF7
  84+ A041 ED 79                out     (c), a
  85+ A043 3E 7C                ld      a, P126_VIDEO_PAGE3
  86+ A045 01 F7 FF             ld      bc, #FFF7
  87+ A048 ED 79                out     (c), a
  88+ A04A
  89+ A04A                      ; LD BC,#0177 / OUT (C),A / JP p126_atm_video_mode_ready
  90+ A04A 21 EC FF             ld      hl, #FFEC
  91+ A04D 36 01                ld      (hl), #01
  92+ A04F 23                   inc     hl
  93+ A050 36 77                ld      (hl), #77
  94+ A052 23                   inc     hl
  95+ A053 36 01                ld      (hl), #01
  96+ A055 23                   inc     hl
  97+ A056 36 ED                ld      (hl), #ED
  98+ A058 23                   inc     hl
  99+ A059 36 79                ld      (hl), #79
 100+ A05B 23                   inc     hl
 101+ A05C 36 C3                ld      (hl), #C3
 102+ A05E 23                   inc     hl
 103+ A05F 36 6E                ld      (hl), p126_atm_video_mode_ready & #FF
 104+ A061 23                   inc     hl
 105+ A062 36 A0                ld      (hl), p126_atm_video_mode_ready >> 8
 106+ A064
 107+ A064                      ; ATM Turbo 2+ RG=6: hardware text 80x25.
 108+ A064 3E 06                ld      a,P126_ATM_VIDEO_MODE_RG6_TEXT80X25
 109+ A066 32 00 A0             ld      (p126_atm_video_mode),a
 110+ A069 3E C6                ld      a, #C6
 111+ A06B C3 EC FF             jp      #FFEC
 112+ A06E
 113+ A06E              p126_atm_video_mode_ready:
 114+ A06E                      ; Restore CPU-visible host/staging pages. Keep D7 clear on #FFF7 so
 115+ A06E                      ; window 3 remains fixed to RAM page 0 for the TR-DOS staging buffer.
 116+ A06E 3E 7A                ld      a, P126_HOST_PAGE5
 117+ A070 01 F7 7F             ld      bc, #7FF7
 118+ A073 ED 79                out     (c), a
 119+ A075 3E 7F                ld      a, P126_HOST_PAGE0
 120+ A077 01 F7 FF             ld      bc, #FFF7
 121+ A07A ED 79                out     (c), a
 122+ A07C
 123+ A07C E1                   pop     hl
 124+ A07D D1                   pop     de
 125+ A07E C1                   pop     bc
 126+ A07F F1                   pop     af
 127+ A080 C9                   ret
 128+ A081
 129+ A081
 130+ A081              ; Reopen the permanent ATM shadow-port/DOSEN state after DS12887 access.
 131+ A081              ; #EFF7 bit 7 is the RTC gate; closing it also closes the permanent DOSEN
 132+ A081              ; path. Re-enter the TR-DOS #0177 service and restore RG6 text mode exactly
 133+ A081              ; as in Project126's atm_host implementation.
 134+ A081              p126_atm_restore_dosen:
 134+ A081
 135+ A081 F5                   push    af
 136+ A082 C5                   push    bc
 137+ A083 01 98 A0             ld      bc,p126_atm_restore_dosen_done
 138+ A086 C5                   push    bc
 139+ A087 01 53 2A             ld      bc,#2A53
 140+ A08A C5                   push    bc
 141+ A08B 01 77 01             ld      bc,#0177
 142+ A08E 3E 06                ld      a,P126_ATM_VIDEO_MODE_RG6_TEXT80X25
 143+ A090 32 00 A0             ld      (p126_atm_video_mode),a
 144+ A093 3E C6                ld      a,#C6
 145+ A095 C3 2F 3D             jp      #3D2F
 146+ A098              p126_atm_restore_dosen_done:
 147+ A098 C1                   pop     bc
 148+ A099 F1                   pop     af
 149+ A09A C9                   ret
 150+ A09B
 151+ A09B
 152+ A09B              p127_ui_init:
 153+ A09B CD 11 A0             call p126_atm_video_init
 154+ A09E CD A8 A0             call BIOS_DebugConsole_Init
 155+ A0A1 CD B8 A0             call BIOS_DebugConsole_Clear
 156+ A0A4 CD 3E A1             call BIOS_DebugConsole_DrawInterface
 157+ A0A7 C9                   ret

 187+ A0D9                      ; Default attributes, then color the status panel blue/white.

 189+ A0DC 11 C1 E1             ld      de, #E1C1
 190+ A0DF 36 47                ld      (hl), #47
 191+ A0E1 01 3F 06             ld      bc, #063F
 192+ A0E4 ED B0                ldir

 197+ A0F1 ED B0                ldir

 201+ A0F9 36 4F                ld      (hl), #4F

 203+ A0FE ED B0                ldir

 206+ A106 36 4F                ld      (hl), #4F
 207+ A108 01 FF 02             ld      bc, #02FF
 208+ A10B ED B0                ldir
 209+ A10D
 210+ A10D                      ; Lower log area: black background, green foreground.
 211+ A10D 21 C0 E4             ld      hl, #E4C0

 213+ A113 36 44                ld      (hl), #44
 214+ A115 01 3F 03             ld      bc, #033F
 215+ A118 ED B0                ldir

 217+ A11D 11 C2 C4             ld      de, #C4C2

 250+ A155 21 E8 A3             ld      hl, p126_ui_line11

 256+ A15F E1                   pop     hl
 257+ A160 C1                   pop     bc
 258+ A161 F1                   pop     af
 259+ A162 C9                   ret

 261+ A163              ; IN: HL=zero-terminated text, A=absolute row 0..24, C=column 0..79.
 262+ A163              ; Every static UI character is mirrored to port #EF.
 263+ A163              BIOS_DebugConsole_PrintAt:
 263+ A163

 265+ A164 C5                   push    bc

 269+ A16A DA D3 A1             jp      c,.p126_print_at_denied
 270+ A16D                      ; The lease routine uses A; restore the complete caller frame before
 271+ A16D                      ; consuming the PrintAt row/column parameters.
 272+ A16D E1                   pop     hl
 273+ A16E D1                   pop     de
 274+ A16F C1                   pop     bc
 275+ A170 F1                   pop     af
 276+ A171 F5                   push    af
 277+ A172 C5                   push    bc
 278+ A173 D5                   push    de
 279+ A174 E5                   push    hl
 280+ A175 22 0D A0             ld      (bios_dbg_text_ptr), hl
 281+ A178 32 0F A0             ld      (bios_dbg_print_row), a
 282+ A17B 79                   ld      a, c
 283+ A17C 32 10 A0             ld      (bios_dbg_print_col), a
 284+ A17F              .p126_print_at_loop:
 285+ A17F 2A 0D A0             ld      hl, (bios_dbg_text_ptr)
 286+ A182 7E                   ld      a, (hl)
 287+ A183 B7                   or      a
 288+ A184 CA C0 A1             jp      z, .p126_print_at_done
 289+ A187 32 06 A0             ld      (bios_dbg_char), a
 290+ A18A 23                   inc     hl
 291+ A18B 22 0D A0             ld      (bios_dbg_text_ptr), hl
 292+ A18E 3A 0F A0             ld      a, (bios_dbg_print_row)
 293+ A191 57                   ld      d, a
 294+ A192 3A 10 A0             ld      a, (bios_dbg_print_col)
 295+ A195 5F                   ld      e, a
 296+ A196 3A 06 A0             ld      a, (bios_dbg_char)
 297+ A199 F5                   push    af
 298+ A19A 01 EF 00             ld      bc,P127_UI_DEBUG_PORT
 299+ A19D ED 79                out     (c),a
 300+ A19F F1                   pop     af
 301+ A1A0 CD 77 A2             call    p126_screen_write_char
 302+ A1A3 3A 10 A0             ld      a, (bios_dbg_print_col)
 303+ A1A6 3C                   inc     a
 304+ A1A7 FE 50                cp      BIOS_DEBUG_COLUMNS
 305+ A1A9 DA BA A1             jp      c, .p126_print_at_col_ok
 306+ A1AC AF                   xor     a
 307+ A1AD 32 10 A0             ld      (bios_dbg_print_col), a
 308+ A1B0 3A 0F A0             ld      a, (bios_dbg_print_row)
 309+ A1B3 3C                   inc     a
 310+ A1B4 32 0F A0             ld      (bios_dbg_print_row), a
 311+ A1B7 C3 7F A1             jp      .p126_print_at_loop
 312+ A1BA              .p126_print_at_col_ok:
 313+ A1BA 32 10 A0             ld      (bios_dbg_print_col), a
 314+ A1BD C3 7F A1             jp      .p126_print_at_loop
 315+ A1C0              .p126_print_at_done:
 316+ A1C0 CD 99 6D             call    p127_w3_ui_leave
 317+ A1C3 01 EF 00             ld      bc,P127_UI_DEBUG_PORT
 318+ A1C6 3E 0D                ld      a,13
 319+ A1C8 ED 79                out     (c),a
 320+ A1CA 3E 0A                ld      a,10
 321+ A1CC ED 79                out     (c),a
 322+ A1CE E1                   pop     hl
 323+ A1CF D1                   pop     de
 324+ A1D0 C1                   pop     bc
 325+ A1D1 F1                   pop     af
 326+ A1D2 C9                   ret
 327+ A1D3              .p126_print_at_denied:
 328+ A1D3 E1                   pop     hl
 329+ A1D4 D1                   pop     de

 331+ A1D6 F1                   pop     af
 332+ A1D7 C9                   ret

 334+ A1D8              ; IN: A=character. CR/LF/BS are handled; printable ASCII is rendered.
 335+ A1D8              BIOS_DebugConsole_PrintChar:
 335+ A1D8

 356+ A1FF FE 80                cp      128

 358+ A204 3E 3F                ld      a, '?'
 359+ A206              .p126_console_char_ok:

 361+ A209 3A 04 A0             ld      a, (bios_dbg_row)

 500+ A2F7              ; IN: A=character, B=ATM-native attribute, D=row, E=column.

   1+ A40C              ; ============================================================================
   2+ A40C              ; Project127 physical ATM RAM page test — Stage 2.23
   3+ A40C              ; Module version: 0.4.3
   4+ A40C              ; Runs from W2 (#A000..), with stack in W2. W1 resident/kernel remains fixed.
   5+ A40C              ; W3 is exclusively leased from the UI and restored to physical page 0.
   6+ A40C              ; This is the first general-purpose physical W3 remap test.
   7+ A40C              ; ============================================================================
   8+ A40C              P127_PHYSMEM_VERSION_MAJOR EQU 0
   9+ A40C              P127_PHYSMEM_VERSION_MINOR EQU 4
  10+ A40C              P127_PHYSMEM_VERSION_PATCH EQU 3
  11+ A40C              P127_PHYSMEM_PAGE_SIZE     EQU 04000h
  12+ A40C              P127_PHYSMEM_WINDOW_BASE   EQU 0C000h
  13+ A40C              P127_PHYSMEM_PAGE_PORT     EQU 0FFF7h
  14+ A40C
  15+ A40C              ; OUT: carry=1 on any failure. Starts with two pages, then allocates and
  16+ A40C              ; scans every remaining free physical page under one W3 ownership lease.
  17+ A40C              ; The probe byte is complemented so it can never equal the saved P0 byte.
  18+ A40C              ; If the dispatcher is not actually switching, only that one P0 byte is
  19+ A40C              ; touched and it is restored before returning. Full-page writes start only
  20+ A40C              ; after the physical switch has been proven.
  21+ A40C              p127_physmem_batch_selftest:
  22+ A40C 3E 53                ld      a,'S'
  23+ A40E CD BA AA             call    p127_physmem_trace
  24+ A411 AF                   xor     a
  25+ A412 32 7D AB             ld      (p127_physmem_error),a
  26+ A415 32 7E AB             ld      (p127_physmem_alloc_count),a
  27+ A418 32 7F AB             ld      (p127_physmem_quiesced),a
  28+ A41B 32 80 AB             ld      (p127_physmem_acquired),a
  29+ A41E 32 81 AB             ld      (p127_physmem_mapped),a
  30+ A421 32 82 AB             ld      (p127_physmem_saved_valid),a
  31+ A424

  66+ A46E
  67+ A46E                      ; Preserve one byte from P0 for a non-destructive port-open probe.
  68+ A46E 3A 00 C0             ld      a,(P127_PHYSMEM_WINDOW_BASE)
  69+ A471 32 86 AB             ld      (p127_physmem_saved_p0),a
  70+ A474 2F                   cpl

  83+ A491

 440+ A77A B7                   or      a

 443+ A77D                      ; mirrored to #EF only. Replay the compact page list now that W3=P0
 444+ A77D                      ; and the UI owner has been restored.

 452+ A784              ; Replay all successful physical-page selections on the visible ATM console.
 453+ A784              ; The 57 IDs are compacted into two wrapped lines rather than 114 long lines.
 454+ A784              p127_physmem_replay_page_log:
 455+ A784                      ; The W3 sweep and its cleanup may leave the video-bank selector in a
 456+ A784                      ; different state even though the CPU windows have been restored.
 457+ A784                      ; Reapply the proven Project126/ATM RG6 setup only after the W3 lease
 458+ A784                      ; is released and UI ownership is active. This preserves screen RAM.

 576+ A8C0              ; Check the seven fixed reservation states: P0/P1/P2/P3/P4/P5/P7.
 577+ A8C0              ; OUT carry=1 if any protected page is not RESERVED.

 648+ A920              ; After release, every non-reserved page must be FREE; reserved pages

 688+ A957              ; Emit one printable phase marker directly on the established #EF debug port.
 689+ A957              ; This remains available while the UI is quiesced and W3 is leased.
 690+ A957
 691+ A957              ; ============================================================================
 692+ A957              ; Stage 2.20 — guarded physical-page API.
 693+ A957              ; All code and stack remain in W2. The only hardware mapping operation is W3.
 694+ A957              ; Public ABI:
 695+ A957              ;   alloc_page: OUT A=allocated page, carry=1 if exhausted.
 696+ A957              ;   free_page:  IN A=allocated page, carry=1 if invalid or lease active.
 697+ A957              ;   w3_begin:  quiesce UI, acquire W3, save P0 byte; DI remains in force.
 698+ A957              ;   w3_map:    IN A=allocated page; valid only inside an active W3 lease.
 699+ A957              ;   w3_end:    restore W3=P0 and saved byte, release lease, resume UI.
 700+ A957              ; No API maps W0/W1/W2, frees a currently mapped page, or enables IRQs.
 701+ A957              ; ============================================================================
 702+ A957              p127_physmem_alloc_page:
 703+ A957 C3 C3 72             jp      p127_mem_alloc
 704+ A95A
 705+ A95A              p127_physmem_free_page:
 706+ A95A 32 B7 AA             ld      (p127_physmem_api_page_arg),a
 707+ A95D 3A B2 AA             ld      a,(p127_physmem_api_active)
 708+ A960 B7                   or      a
 709+ A961 C2 14 AA             jp      nz,p127_physmem_api_reject
 710+ A964 3A B7 AA             ld      a,(p127_physmem_api_page_arg)
 711+ A967 47                   ld      b,a
 712+ A968 3A 38 82             ld      a,(p127_map_shadow+3)
 713+ A96B B8                   cp      b
 714+ A96C CA 14 AA             jp      z,p127_physmem_api_reject
 715+ A96F 3A B7 AA             ld      a,(p127_physmem_api_page_arg)
 716+ A972 C3 D9 72             jp      p127_mem_free
 717+ A975
 718+ A975              ; Begin a bounded W3 lease. Bootstrap mapping must be W3=P0.
 719+ A975              p127_physmem_w3_begin:
 720+ A975 F3                   di
 721+ A976 3A B2 AA             ld      a,(p127_physmem_api_active)
 722+ A979 B7                   or      a
 723+ A97A C2 14 AA             jp      nz,p127_physmem_api_reject
 724+ A97D 3A 38 82             ld      a,(p127_map_shadow+3)
 725+ A980 B7                   or      a
 726+ A981 C2 14 AA             jp      nz,p127_physmem_api_reject
 727+ A984 CD 9E 6D             call    p127_w3_quiesce
 728+ A987 D8                   ret     c
 729+ A988 3E 01                ld      a,1
 730+ A98A 32 B3 AA             ld      (p127_physmem_api_quiesced),a
 731+ A98D CD BD 6D             call    p127_w3_handoff_acquire
 732+ A990 38 15                jr      c,p127_physmem_w3_begin_rollback
 733+ A992 3E 01                ld      a,1
 734+ A994 32 B4 AA             ld      (p127_physmem_api_acquired),a
 735+ A997 3A 00 C0             ld      a,(P127_PHYSMEM_WINDOW_BASE)
 736+ A99A 32 B6 AA             ld      (p127_physmem_api_saved_p0),a
 737+ A99D 3E 01                ld      a,1
 738+ A99F 32 B5 AA             ld      (p127_physmem_api_saved_valid),a
 739+ A9A2 32 B2 AA             ld      (p127_physmem_api_active),a
 740+ A9A5 B7                   or      a
 741+ A9A6 C9                   ret
 742+ A9A7              p127_physmem_w3_begin_rollback:
 743+ A9A7 CD E5 6D             call    p127_w3_resume
 744+ A9AA AF                   xor     a
 745+ A9AB 32 B3 AA             ld      (p127_physmem_api_quiesced),a
 746+ A9AE 37                   scf
 747+ A9AF C9                   ret
 748+ A9B0
 749+ A9B0              ; Map only an allocator-owned page into W3.
 750+ A9B0              p127_physmem_w3_map:
 751+ A9B0 32 B7 AA             ld      (p127_physmem_api_page_arg),a
 752+ A9B3 3A B2 AA             ld      a,(p127_physmem_api_active)
 753+ A9B6 FE 01                cp      1
 754+ A9B8 C2 14 AA             jp      nz,p127_physmem_api_reject
 755+ A9BB 3A B7 AA             ld      a,(p127_physmem_api_page_arg)
 756+ A9BE FE 40                cp      P127_PHYSMEM_MAX_PAGES
 757+ A9C0 D2 14 AA             jp      nc,p127_physmem_api_reject
 758+ A9C3 5F                   ld      e,a
 759+ A9C4 16 00                ld      d,0
 760+ A9C6 21 84 73             ld      hl,p127_mem_page_state
 761+ A9C9 19                   add     hl,de
 762+ A9CA 7E                   ld      a,(hl)
 763+ A9CB FE 02                cp      P127_MEM_ALLOCATED
 764+ A9CD C2 14 AA             jp      nz,p127_physmem_api_reject
 765+ A9D0 3A B7 AA             ld      a,(p127_physmem_api_page_arg)
 766+ A9D3 C3 40 AB             jp      p127_physmem_set_w3
 767+ A9D6
 768+ A9D6              ; End the lease in recovery order. If release/resume fails, state flags are
 769+ A9D6              ; retained so the caller can retry this routine without losing ownership.
 770+ A9D6              p127_physmem_w3_end:
 771+ A9D6 3A B2 AA             ld      a,(p127_physmem_api_active)
 772+ A9D9 FE 01                cp      1
 773+ A9DB C2 14 AA             jp      nz,p127_physmem_api_reject
 774+ A9DE AF                   xor     a
 775+ A9DF CD 40 AB             call    p127_physmem_set_w3
 776+ A9E2 D8                   ret     c
 777+ A9E3 3A B5 AA             ld      a,(p127_physmem_api_saved_valid)
 778+ A9E6 B7                   or      a
 779+ A9E7 28 06                jr      z,p127_physmem_w3_end_release
 780+ A9E9 3A B6 AA             ld      a,(p127_physmem_api_saved_p0)
 781+ A9EC 32 00 C0             ld      (P127_PHYSMEM_WINDOW_BASE),a
 782+ A9EF              p127_physmem_w3_end_release:
 783+ A9EF 3A B4 AA             ld      a,(p127_physmem_api_acquired)
 784+ A9F2 B7                   or      a
 785+ A9F3 28 08                jr      z,p127_physmem_w3_end_resume
 786+ A9F5 CD D1 6D             call    p127_w3_handoff_release
 787+ A9F8 D8                   ret     c
 788+ A9F9 AF                   xor     a
 789+ A9FA 32 B4 AA             ld      (p127_physmem_api_acquired),a
 790+ A9FD              p127_physmem_w3_end_resume:
 791+ A9FD 3A B3 AA             ld      a,(p127_physmem_api_quiesced)
 792+ AA00 B7                   or      a
 793+ AA01 28 08                jr      z,p127_physmem_w3_end_done
 794+ AA03 CD E5 6D             call    p127_w3_resume
 795+ AA06 D8                   ret     c
 796+ AA07 AF                   xor     a
 797+ AA08 32 B3 AA             ld      (p127_physmem_api_quiesced),a
 798+ AA0B              p127_physmem_w3_end_done:
 799+ AA0B AF                   xor     a
 800+ AA0C 32 B5 AA             ld      (p127_physmem_api_saved_valid),a
 801+ AA0F 32 B2 AA             ld      (p127_physmem_api_active),a
 802+ AA12 B7                   or      a
 803+ AA13 C9                   ret
 804+ AA14
 805+ AA14              p127_physmem_api_reject:
 806+ AA14 37                   scf
 807+ AA15 C9                   ret
 808+ AA16
 809+ AA16              ; Runtime API contract test. It proves rejection paths, allocated-page-only
 810+ AA16              ; mapping, mapped-page free protection, P0 byte restoration and UI recovery.
 811+ AA16              p127_physmem_api_selftest:
 812+ AA16 AF                   xor     a
 813+ AA17 32 B2 AA             ld      (p127_physmem_api_active),a
 814+ AA1A 32 B3 AA             ld      (p127_physmem_api_quiesced),a
 815+ AA1D 32 B4 AA             ld      (p127_physmem_api_acquired),a
 816+ AA20 32 B5 AA             ld      (p127_physmem_api_saved_valid),a
 817+ AA23 3E 01                ld      a,1
 818+ AA25 CD 5A A9             call    p127_physmem_free_page
 819+ AA28 D2 B0 AA             jp      nc,p127_physmem_api_test_fail
 820+ AA2B CD 57 A9             call    p127_physmem_alloc_page
 821+ AA2E DA B0 AA             jp      c,p127_physmem_api_test_fail
 822+ AA31 32 B8 AA             ld      (p127_physmem_api_test_page),a
 823+ AA34 3A 00 C0             ld      a,(P127_PHYSMEM_WINDOW_BASE)
 824+ AA37 32 B9 AA             ld      (p127_physmem_api_test_p0),a
 825+ AA3A CD 75 A9             call    p127_physmem_w3_begin
 826+ AA3D DA AA AA             jp      c,p127_physmem_api_test_free
 827+ AA40                      ; A reserved page must never be accepted by the map API.
 828+ AA40 3E 01                ld      a,1
 829+ AA42 CD B0 A9             call    p127_physmem_w3_map
 830+ AA45 D2 A7 AA             jp      nc,p127_physmem_api_test_end
 831+ AA48 3A B8 AA             ld      a,(p127_physmem_api_test_page)
 832+ AA4B CD B0 A9             call    p127_physmem_w3_map
 833+ AA4E DA A7 AA             jp      c,p127_physmem_api_test_end
 834+ AA51                      ; The currently mapped page cannot be freed while the lease is held.
 835+ AA51 3A B8 AA             ld      a,(p127_physmem_api_test_page)
 836+ AA54 CD 5A A9             call    p127_physmem_free_page
 837+ AA57 D2 A7 AA             jp      nc,p127_physmem_api_test_end
 838+ AA5A 3E A5                ld      a,0A5h
 839+ AA5C 32 00 C0             ld      (P127_PHYSMEM_WINDOW_BASE),a
 840+ AA5F CD D6 A9             call    p127_physmem_w3_end
 841+ AA62 DA B0 AA             jp      c,p127_physmem_api_test_fail
 842+ AA65 3A 00 C0             ld      a,(P127_PHYSMEM_WINDOW_BASE)
 843+ AA68 47                   ld      b,a
 844+ AA69 3A B9 AA             ld      a,(p127_physmem_api_test_p0)
 845+ AA6C B8                   cp      b
 846+ AA6D C2 B0 AA             jp      nz,p127_physmem_api_test_fail
 847+ AA70 3A B8 AA             ld      a,(p127_physmem_api_test_page)
 848+ AA73 CD 5A A9             call    p127_physmem_free_page
 849+ AA76 DA B0 AA             jp      c,p127_physmem_api_test_fail
 850+ AA79                      ; First-fit must return the same page after release.
 851+ AA79 CD 57 A9             call    p127_physmem_alloc_page
 852+ AA7C DA B0 AA             jp      c,p127_physmem_api_test_fail
 853+ AA7F 47                   ld      b,a
 854+ AA80 3A B8 AA             ld      a,(p127_physmem_api_test_page)
 855+ AA83 B8                   cp      b
 856+ AA84 C2 B0 AA             jp      nz,p127_physmem_api_test_fail
 857+ AA87 78                   ld      a,b
 858+ AA88 CD 5A A9             call    p127_physmem_free_page
 859+ AA8B DA B0 AA             jp      c,p127_physmem_api_test_fail
 860+ AA8E 3A 0B 6F             ld      a,(p127_w3_state)
 861+ AA91 FE 01                cp      P127_W3_STATE_UI_ACTIVE
 862+ AA93 C2 B0 AA             jp      nz,p127_physmem_api_test_fail
 863+ AA96 3A 0C 6F             ld      a,(p127_w3_owner)
 864+ AA99 FE 01                cp      P127_W3_OWNER_UI
 865+ AA9B C2 B0 AA             jp      nz,p127_physmem_api_test_fail
 866+ AA9E 3A 38 82             ld      a,(p127_map_shadow+3)
 867+ AAA1 B7                   or      a
 868+ AAA2 C2 B0 AA             jp      nz,p127_physmem_api_test_fail
 869+ AAA5 B7                   or      a
 870+ AAA6 C9                   ret
 871+ AAA7
 872+ AAA7              p127_physmem_api_test_end:
 873+ AAA7 CD D6 A9             call    p127_physmem_w3_end
 874+ AAAA              p127_physmem_api_test_free:
 875+ AAAA 3A B8 AA             ld      a,(p127_physmem_api_test_page)
 876+ AAAD CD 5A A9             call    p127_physmem_free_page
 877+ AAB0              p127_physmem_api_test_fail:
 878+ AAB0 37                   scf
 879+ AAB1 C9                   ret
 880+ AAB2
 881+ AAB2 00           p127_physmem_api_active: db 0
 882+ AAB3 00           p127_physmem_api_quiesced: db 0
 883+ AAB4 00           p127_physmem_api_acquired: db 0
 884+ AAB5 00           p127_physmem_api_saved_valid: db 0
 885+ AAB6 00           p127_physmem_api_saved_p0: db 0

 887+ AAB8 00           p127_physmem_api_test_page: db 0
 888+ AAB9 00           p127_physmem_api_test_p0: db 0
